## ROUND 2 — the owner's "do all the things you listed, then a full bug test, re-planned from nothing" (2026-10-15) · P181501–P181952

**Asked:** *"do all the things that you have listed right now … do a full bug test … check every single bit of thing. So
replan whole test. Don't use the previous one."* Items 6–12 were done first (item 9 had already been fixed and merged by T16
on 2026-10-13, and is proved live below). The round-1 blocks were NOT re-used: this plan is new, in
`scripts/sweep/t12r2/` (`node scripts/sweep/t12r2/run.mjs`), and is built to leave nothing out by ENUMERATING the code
rather than choosing from it:

* **G1 · every function** — all 198 top-level functions and arrows in app.js, each proved defined in the running page and
  called by something (no dead code) · **G2 · every data-\* attribute** the panel writes or reads, each with a reader and a
  writer · **G3 · every sentence the panel toasts**, each plain words with no code in it.
* **P · every asset** index.html loads (served, right type, ?v= its real hash), **every id** it declares, the **host pages**
  as French House's waiter opens them, and **what the waiter's browser receives** (only French House, no secret field,
  tiles not order rows, a table's detail only that table).
* **L · the states round 1 never visited** — a waiter WITH a section, the admin's X-ray, the server misbehaving (500 / 409 /
  wrong PIN / a blocked device / a busy first load — stand-ins at the panel's own helpers), opened with NO internet, two
  waiters on one dish in the same instant, the back button across chained boxes, this round's fixes live, a whole service
  on a phone with real taps only, the keyboard and the screen reader, a manager's change arriving, and what an idle or
  hidden tablet costs.

**Rows by file:** app.js 399 (70 driven) · index.html 40 (25) · app/r/[restaurant]/tablet/page.tsx 6 (5) ·
app/tablet/page.tsx 5 (5) · app/tablet/layout.tsx 1 · lib/clash.ts 1. **452 rows — 452 ✅ · 0 ❌ · 0 ⏭** on the final run.

**What it found (each fixed, its own commit):** a dead hook on the Print bill button left since R62 (P181708, by G2), and no
box on the tablet telling a screen reader it is a dialog (P181947, by L9). Round 1's two outside-territory ❌ are closed:
P181345's back-button drift by T16's merged fix (proved by L6), P181166's same-instant saves by item 10 (proved by L5).

**Detector mistakes, recorded so nobody re-files them:** a bare `data-x>` attribute counted as "never written"; an
obituary comment quoting a removed hook counted as the hook; a toast assembled wholly from the WHY_SAYS sentences counted
as "too short"; a GET stand-in placed in front of the service worker (it never saw the request); a PIN typed before the
box's own 50 ms focus; a "manager's order arrives" check started on a table that was already busy; ALL-CAPS ribbon text
matched case-sensitively. **And one of MINE, on shared data:** while stopping a stuck run I retired tables by hand
including 29 and 30, whose two empty sessions belonged to someone else; both were put back exactly (status open, the
closed/deleted stamps cleared) within minutes, by id. The harness itself only ever retires the two tables it picked.

## ROUND 3 — the owner's "do it again, replan the whole thing … completely error free … every single bit" (2026-10-16) · P213001–P213999

**Asked** (with item 25 from T16's report): *"do it again, maybe replan whole thing and do it again because maybe some error
will be left. I want it completely error free and replan whole test, like everything should be covered, every single bit."*
The ID block was claimed on `main` first (PR #1528, owner's yes). Plan: `scripts/sweep/t12r3/PLAN.md`. Rounds 1 and 2 were
lists someone wrote; round 3 is planned from what the machine REPORTS:

* **C · coverage** (`cov.mjs`) — rounds 1 + 2 together ran **60.2%** of app.js (2,212 / 3,676 lines). That list drove band F.
* **F · the fixture world** (`f-world.mjs`, `f-record.mjs`, `fixtures/`) — the panel against a server the TEST owns, built
  from real recorded answers: any restaurant, any answer (refusal, clash, 5xx, hang, blocked device, offline), nothing
  written anywhere. Groups FA–FK below. **F alone runs 94.7%; everything together on the final code runs 99.2%**
  (3,656 / 3,685). The 29 lines left: the admin ribbon's exits (they leave the page before its coverage is written — FF
  proves each), two timers (the 10-minute menu self-heal, the print callback), a bell error warning and an unreachable catch.
* **T · the three host pages** (FT) — read-only on the real pages, as the waiter and as the admin.
* **M · mutation testing** (`mutate.mjs`) — one small break at a time in a COPY of app.js, served in its place, judged by the
  groups that execute that line, after an unchanged control copy passes. Rows P213993–P213999.
* **R · regression** — every round-1 and round-2 row re-run on the final code and stamped in place above (`↻ S10-T12 round 3`).
  Round 1's block B (P181201–P181345) could NOT be re-run on the final code: it needs four free tables and French House's floor
  held only two in eight tries (other terminals' leftover binned sessions and live runs); it passed 145/145 that morning.
* **Item 25** (`dash-charts.mjs`) — the manager Dashboard's seven charts against real bills. Rows P213988–P213992.

**Found and fixed — ten, one commit each, each guarded in `verify:tablet-taps` (every guard fails on the old code):**
1 closing an already-free table said "Couldn't check … try again" · 2 loyalty points offered below the restaurant's minimum ·
3 "Use points" said "undefined points used" offline and nothing when refused · 4 a table type picked offline said "Marked 👑 VIP" ·
5 a refused tip read "…here.. Add it" · 6 a tip typed on the pay sheet was lost on "Split this bill →" (round 2's item 5 was
incomplete) · 7 a dish edit saved offline said "Dish updated" · 8 banquet "Create the bill" with no plates swallowed the tap ·
9 a sectioned waiter's guest bell listed other sections' calls (round 1's P181200 had matched the line's text) ·
10 the drawer's clock showed the tablet's time zone, not the restaurant's.
